# Claims ledger summary (S23)

Generated by `tools/class_claims.py`; do not edit by hand. The ledger itself is `data/claims.csv`: one row per figure per sentence, on every live page, Markdown page, PDF and `data/optimal-parameters.json` (the `/v1/` archive is not included).

## What this is

Every number the Hub states (percentages, money, multipliers, counts, durations, years ahead) was extracted by `tools/extract_claims.py` and given one basis class from research rule 5, and an action for the corrections step (S24) and the rewrite (S25):

- **keep**: the figure has the basis its class names;
- **correct**: the figure or its arithmetic is wrong, or its source must be added (the note says what);
- **relabel**: keep the number but say what it is, usually an illustrative projection, a design parameter or a target;
- **remove**: nothing supports it and nothing honest can be said in its place. Most removals are model results that no published run produced: S30 looks for the code that may have made them, and S25 replaces them with results from the preregistered studies.

Each class was set by a rule in `tools/claims_review.py` written after reading the passage (the `rule` column names it), or by the reference list (`ref`), or, for a PDF figure that also appears on the paper's HTML page, by the class reviewed there (`html:R…`). Reviewed or reference-list classes: 6661 of 6661; keyword fallbacks: 0.

How to read the classes: a value the framework chooses is a **design parameter**; arithmetic that follows from figures printed beside it is **derived** (`checked` says whether it was recomputed); a real-world figure is an **external source** only when the text cites one (`checked` says whether it was opened); a figure stated as a fact or a result with no source, run or derivation is **no basis found** (`claimed` says what it is presented as). This is Claude's reading of rule 5, taking the cautious reading where a page is unclear.

Figures: **6661** on 55 pages and PDFs. 1571 of them are in a PDF only (the PDF is an older or longer version of its HTML page; S24 rebuilds the PDFs).

On the front page, research summary, paper index, wiki and parameters file (521 figures): derived 65, external source 11, design parameter 270, illustrative projection 28, corpus metadata 51, no basis found 96.

Simulation: only the BLEI paper cites a simulation release for its figures (v4.4, Full Integration, N = 5,000), and it gives no command, seeds or results file, so those rows are marked to be rerun on a release with a recorded command (S40).

External sources: cited, not opened 158, opened 32, named in the text, not opened 16, a standard fact or definition, not cited 11. Opening the rest is left to S24/S25, figure by figure, before any is kept in a rewritten paper. Sources opened this step:

- Greenwald (2009, Cato); ONDCP critique (ojp.gov/pdffiles1/ondcp/Portugal.pdf): opened: ONDCP critique (Greenwald's PDF could not be fetched)
- Wikipedia 'Bristol pound', citing the Bristol Post (9 Dec 2017): opened: 'By late 2017, five million Bristol pounds had been spent'
- uncited; ONDCP critique (ojp.gov/pdffiles1/ondcp/Portugal.pdf) for the deaths series: opened: ONDCP critique

Earlier records merged: the session-5 claims records (S5-01, S5-02, S5-03, S5-04, S5-05, S5-06, S5-07, S5-08, S5-09, S5-10, S5-11, S5-12, S5-13) are carried in `prior_id` (the original claims-ledger draft was not brought over). S29 refine.ink ids are in `s29_id`: 69 ids are linked to figure rows. Valid headline or derivation items with no figure row to attach to (they concern formulas or wording; see the S29 triage): A01, A02, A03, A04, A05, A06, B001, B006, B013, B014, B017, B019, B023, B025, B031, B034, B037, B050, B055, B058, B066, B067, B070, B076, B092, B101, B108.
